## 25. Phase 5p additions (serving efficiency)

Source: `.procoder/specs/phase-5p-serving-efficiency.md` (cited "P5p S-n"), decision "Phase 5p: serving efficiency interlude (SGLang-inspired items) (2026-09-28)" and the provisional choices of "Phase 5p spec: provisional design choices (2026-09-28)". Builds on the Phase 5 names (`TpExecutor`, `StepPlan.copies`, `KvFormat.shards`) as merged. No kernel ABI change.

Configuration (P5p S-11; `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields, default)]` as every config struct):

| Key                                         | Rust field                                            | Default | Validation                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `kv.partial_prefix_reuse`                   | `KvConfig.partial_prefix_reuse: bool`                 | `true`  | —; ignored when `kv.prefix_sharing` is false                    |
| `scheduler.cache_aware_window`              | `SchedulerConfig.cache_aware_window: HumanDuration`   | `1s`    | 10 ms ..= 60 s                                                  |
| `structured_output.jump_forward`            | `StructuredOutputConfig.jump_forward: bool`           | `true`  | —                                                               |
| `structured_output.jump_forward_max_tokens` | `StructuredOutputConfig.jump_forward_max_tokens: u32` | `32`    | 1 ..= 256 and value + 1 ≤ `scheduler.max_batch_tokens` (exit 2) |

`turbine-kv` (P5p S-4, S-5):

```rust
// directory
pub struct PartialMatch { pub key: KvKey, pub block: BlockId, pub tokens: u32 }
// PrefixMatch gains: pub partial: Option<PartialMatch>
impl KvDirectory { pub fn insert_partial(&mut self, block: KvBlock) -> Result<(), DirectoryError>; }   // L0-only partial entry; LRU beyond PARTIAL_PER_PARENT
impl KvBlock { pub fn is_partial(&self, block_tokens: u32) -> bool; }
pub const PARTIAL_SCAN_LIMIT: usize = 64; pub const PARTIAL_PER_PARENT: usize = 8;
// planner
// PlanInputs gains: pub partial_tokens: u32;  KvPlan gains: pub partial_tokens: u32
pub fn reuse_cap_tokens(prompt_tokens: u32) -> u32;          // prompt − MIN_RECOMPUTE_TOKENS (still 2)
// hierarchy
pub struct TailCopy { pub src: BlockId, pub dst: BlockId, pub tokens: u32 }
// PrefixAttach gains: pub tail_copy: Option<TailCopy>   (cached_tokens = blocks × block_tokens + tail tokens)
impl KvHierarchy {
    pub fn publish_tail(&mut self, pool: &mut BlockPool, request: RequestId, block: BlockId, tokens: &[u32]);
    pub fn tail_copied(&mut self, pool: &mut BlockPool, request: RequestId);
}
// HierarchyConfig gains: pub partial_prefix_reuse: bool
// metrics
#[non_exhaustive] pub enum PartialOutcome { Reused, NoCandidate, NotResident, PlannerCutoff, Disabled }   // "reused", "no_candidate", "not_resident", "planner_cutoff", "disabled"
// EvictReason gains PartialL0Only ("partial_l0_only")
```

Partial entries never enter L1/L2, prefetch or promotion; `has_reuse_evidence` is false for them. Under TP a tail copy is one logical `(src, dst)` pair executed on every rank through `TpExecutor::copy_blocks` / `StepPlan.copies`, like a fork copy.

`turbine-scheduler` (P5p S-5, S-7, S-9):

```rust
// IterationPlan gains: pub tail_copies: Vec<(SeqId, BlockId, BlockId)>   (executed before the forward, after forks)
// IterationOutcome gains: pub rolled_back: Vec<(SeqId, u32)>            (tokens removed from the table's end)
impl Scheduler { pub fn extend(&mut self, seq: SeqId, tokens: u32) -> Result<(), SchedError>; }   // SchedError::NotDecoding
// SchedulerParams gains: pub cache_aware_window: Duration
// policy::AdmissionInfo gains: pub prompt_tokens: u32, pub cached_prefix_tokens: u32, pub window: Duration
// policy::AdmissionKey becomes { tier: u8, priority: Priority, arrival: Duration, affinity: u64, order: u64 }   (DefaultPolicy: affinity 0)
pub struct CacheAwarePolicy;   // name "cache_aware"; key: tier 1, priority, arrival window start, affinity u32::MAX − cached_prefix_tokens, order submit_no
// sim: KvSimDriver::with_policy(&'static dyn SchedulingPolicy); SimReport gains pub admission_inversions: Vec<(RequestId, RequestId)>
```

`turbine-model` (P5p S-9, S-10): `SeqSlice.logits_rows: u32` (1 ≤ rows ≤ `q_len`; rows > 1 are full rows, never device-reduced); `Logits::rows_of(&self, seq_index: usize) -> &[f32]`; `structured::TokenMatcher::forced_tokens(&mut self, max: usize) -> Vec<u32>` (default empty; `LlguidanceMatcher` over llguidance's `Matcher::compute_ff_tokens`, commits nothing).

`turbine-server` (P5p S-10): `engine::requests::ForcedOutcome { accepted: u32, next: Option<SampledToken>, finished: Option<FinishReason> }` and `ActiveRequest::step_forced(..)` (contract-chosen names; engine-internal).

`turbine-bench` (P5p S-1): subcommand `turbine-bench stage-share --before <file> --after <file> [--output text|json]` (`turbine_bench::stage_share::{StageShare { iterations, seconds, host_share }, stage_share(before, after), StageShareError}`; exit 0, 2 on a missing histogram or no iterations).

Metrics (P5p S-12; labels from closed enums): `turbine_kv_partial_reuse_total{outcome}` (outcome ∈ `PartialOutcome`), `turbine_kv_partial_reused_tokens_total`, `turbine_kv_partial_entries` (gauge), `turbine_jump_forward_tokens_total{outcome}` (`accepted`, `rejected`), `turbine_jump_forward_steps_total`; `turbine_kv_evictions_total{reason}` gains `partial_l0_only`.

Log events: `kv_partial_reuse` (DEBUG), `jump_forward` (DEBUG; reason `all_accepted` | `model_disagreed` | `stopped`).

Diagnostics: `GET /turbine/v1/kv` gains `partial_prefix_reuse` (bool) and `tiers[l0].partial_entries`.

Lab: `scripts/lab-bench.sh` gains `--dry-run`, `--concurrency <n>`, `--multi-turn`, `--multi-turn-sessions <n>`; the `BENCH` line gains `conc=`, `host_share=` and, with a multi-turn flag, `mt_cached=`, `mt_ttft_first_p50=`, `mt_ttft_first_p99=`, `mt_ttft_later_p50=`, `mt_tok_s=`.

Tests (P5p; `ign` = lab): `turbine-bench --test bench stage_share_from_metrics`, `--test lab_scripts lab_bench_dry_run_passes_flags`; `turbine-core config::tests::phase5p_keys`; `turbine-kv directory::tests::token_granular_match`, `planner::tests::token_granular_cap`; `turbine-scheduler --test kv_sim partial_tail_reuse`, `cancellation_before_tail_copy`, `cache_aware_starvation_bound`, `policy::tests::cache_aware_orders_by_window_then_prefix`, `scheduler::tests::extend_and_roll_back`; `turbine-model --test tiny_model multi_row_logits_match_single_steps`, `structured::tests::forced_tokens_follow_llguidance`; `turbine-server engine::r#loop::tests::partial_prefix_reuse_matches_cold`, (conditional) `engine::r#loop::tests::overlap_waits_for_new_prefills`, `--test tiny_server tp2_partial_prefix_reuse`, `jump_forward_matches_token_by_token`, `--test kv_gpu prefix_reuse_partial_block_matches_cold` (ign), `--test lab_openai json_schema_jump_forward_matches` (ign).

Kernel ABI v2.10 (6a Task 28a, user decision 2026-09-29 "YaRN attention factor: on cos/sin"): header `turbine_rope_desc` gains the trailing `float attn_factor` (read at minor ≥ 10; 1.0 = none), `TURBINE_ABI_MINOR 10u`, no op code or symbol; Rust `RopeContext.attn_factor: f32`, `RopeKernel::attn_factor_supported()` (default false; cpu-reference true, shim = `ShimLibrary::rope_attn_factor()`, minor ≥ 10), `ffi::RopeDesc.attn_factor`, `V21Symbols.rope_attn_factor`; `ModelArchConfig::rope_attention_factor() -> f32` (YaRN's factor, else 1.0), `DecoderDims.rope_attn_factor`, `ModelArchConfig::attention_scale` back to `head_dim^-0.5` for every RoPE; startup refusal `rope_attn_factor_unavailable` (exit 1, `event="kernel_capability"`); stub `TURBINE_STUB_GFX942_V210` (minor 10; `TURBINE_STUB_GFX942_V29` reports 9); `libturbine_hip.so` reports minor 10. The 6b groups first written as v2.10 renumber to v2.11.

Status and metrics (6a Task 25): `/turbine/v1/status` `quantization { weight_format, packaging, activation, kv_dtype, layers }`; `turbine_weight_format_info{format,packaging}`; log events `weight_format` (INFO) and `quant_refused` (ERROR, `reason`); the support key's weight column is the detected packaging's (`support_startup::weight_column`); `amd/gfx1201/LlamaForCausalLM/<phase-6a format>/bf16/none` experimental during each proof, `cpu/*/*/<format>/{bf16,fp8_e4m3}/none` experimental.

TurboQuant L0 pages on the CPU backend (6b Task 11, host side; `p6b-t11b` 45d2e2c): `turbine_core::types::DType::{Tq4, Tq2}` (ABI codes 18, 19; `size_bytes` 1; `DType::tq_record_bytes` → 144 / 80 bytes per token and KV head; `KvLayout::{layer_bytes_per_token, layer_block_bytes}` size TurboQuant pages by record); `turbine_core::config::KvDtypeChoice::{Tq4, Tq2}` (`kv.dtype: tq4|tq2`); `turbine_core::model_identity::KvDtype::{Tq4, Tq2}` (wire codes 4, 5). Kernels: `turbine_kernels::{KV_FMT_BF16 0, KV_FMT_FP8_E4M3 1, KV_FMT_TQ4 2, KV_FMT_TQ2 3, kv_format_code(DType), TqHeadTables { k_signs, v_signs }, TqParams { heads, codebooks }, TqEncodeFn, TqPaged { params, encode }}`; `PagedAttentionContext.{block_formats: &[u8] (empty = every block in cfg.dtype), tq: Option<TqPaged>}`; the attention config of TurboQuant pages names the page dtype; the shim provider refuses TurboQuant pages and mixed-format tables until ABI v2.11 (Task 12). Model: `turbine_model::kv_scales::{TqKv, KvCache::turboquant, KvCache::tq_paged(layer)}`. Server: `turbine-server/src/kv_tq.rs` (`seed(identity, layout)` = first 8 bytes of the unsalted namespace key, `layer_params`, `encode`, `kv_cache`, `dtype`); refusals `kv_tq_unavailable` (a GPU library without the ABI v2.11 group; TP / EP / PP; head_dim ≠ 128) and `kv_transcode_unavailable` (a lower-tier format other than `l0` under TurboQuant L0). Support rows `cpu/*/*/bf16/{tq4,tq2}/none` experimental. Every L0 block of the pool's base format until the ladder's L0 step (Task 17), which adds per-entry format tags in the block table and class-page addressing. Tests: `turbine-model --test tiny_model tq_kv_matches_reference`, `turbine-kernels cpu::paged::tests::mixed_formats_append_and_read_by_tag`, `turbine-server --test tiny_server tq_kv_serves_on_cpu`.

Lossy lineage and reuse (6b Task 4, `p6b-t4`; user decisions 2026-09-30): `turbine_kv::identity::lossy_key(key: KvKey, format: &str, seed: u64) -> KvKey` (BLAKE3("lossy" ‖ key ‖ format ‖ seed)[..16]), `NamespaceKey::seed()`; `turbine_kv::directory::{Lineage::{Exact, Lossy { format }}, format_is_lossy(format, &KvLayout)}`, `KvBlock.lineage`, `MatchedBlock.lossy: Option<&'static str>`, `PrefixMatch.{denied, lossy_from: Option<(usize, &'static str)>, lossy_chain: Vec<KvKey>}` and `PrefixMatch::keys_for(cut) -> (&[KvKey], Lineage)`, `KvDirectory::lookup(hasher, tokens, block_tokens, now, allow_lossy: bool, seed: u64)`. Chain rule: while an exact copy exists a lossy copy is a location on the exact entry; only an L0 copy promoted from a lossy copy gets its own entry under `lossy_key`, and later blocks chain from it. `turbine_kv::planner::PlanInputs.{copy_bytes: &[u64] (encoded bytes per block — transfers are priced at the compressed size), lossy_penalty: &[Option<f64>], allow_lossy: bool}`; the path estimates it multiplies (`TransferEngine::estimate` → `PathCost`) are latency plus a rate per encoded (moved) byte: every completed copy is observed at `TransferRequest.bytes`, its bandwidth sample net of the latency estimate (p6b-t4t15 reconcile). A copy's duration is `TransferBackend::took(&TransferTicket) -> Option<turbine_kv::transfer::CopyTime>`: `CopyTime::Exact(Duration)` when the backend times the copy itself (I/O pool, the simulator's modelled latency + bytes / bandwidth), `CopyTime::Within { at_least, at_most }` when it sees the completion only at a poll (a copy ending on a GPU copy stream: from the last poll that saw a stage running to the poll that saw it done); `None` lets the engine bound it from its own polls. A bounded copy is folded in as the path's unloaded cost (the startup calibration `TransferEngine::seed`, else the fallback) clamped to its bounds: a copy seen running longer raises the estimate, one done within a poll that the unloaded cost fits pulls it back (decision "6b Task 6", point 3, `p6b-planner`). Follow-ups (decision "6b planner follow-ups", 1 A and 2 A, `p6b-planner2`): `planner::plan_cost` charges each path's latency once per plan, shared by the plan's blocks on that path (each share weighted by the block's `1 + lossy_penalty`), and every block's bytes over the path's rate; `TransferEngine` starts queued promotions and prefetches before background copies (`Demote`, `Compress`) and hands background L0 ↔ L1 copies to the copy stream only while its projected backlog (copies at their paths' unloaded costs, one after another) is below the time since the previous pump, at most `transfer::BACKGROUND_WINDOW_MAX` (250 ms), keeping room under `max_inflight_bytes` for the first queued background copy while none runs; `TransferEngine::estimate` decays latency and time per byte that are slower than the unloaded cost toward it with half-life `transfer::ESTIMATE_RECOVERY_HALF_LIFE` (5 s) since the path's last sample. In `static` rank mode the leader's part of a copy is timed by its own `CopyStreamBackend` (`CopyTime` combined with the slowest worker's `TierAck::took_ns`). The eviction score prices a copy at its encoded size too (user decision 2026-09-30, B): `BlockScoreInputs.block.size_bytes` is the copy's bytes in its tier's format and `retrieval` the path estimate for the bytes it would be stored at in the tier below; `make_room` counts spills already leaving a tier as room on the way (user decision 2026-09-30, A). `HierarchyConfig.{allow_lossy, lossy_penalty: Vec<(&'static str, f64)>}` (from `kv.lossy_reuse`, `kv.lossy_penalty`), `AttachRequest.allow_lossy: Option<bool>`, `PrefixAttach.lossy_tokens`. `turbine_core::request::{RequestKvPolicy { allow_lossy }, GenerationRequest.kv_policy: Option<RequestKvPolicy>, Usage.lossy_cached_tokens}`; `turbine_api::kv::{KV_LOSSY = "x-turbine-kv-lossy", parse_kv_lossy}` (`allow` | `deny`, else 400 `invalid_request`); `usage.prompt_tokens_details.lossy_cached_tokens`; counters `turbine_kv_lossy_cached_tokens_total`, `turbine_kv_lossy_denied_total`.

Compression ladder in L1/L2 (6b Task 15, `p6b-t15`): `turbine_kv::hierarchy::{LadderConfig { max_format, high_water, low_water, dwell }, HierarchyConfig.ladder: Option<LadderConfig>}`, `KvHierarchy::{ladder_tick(&mut self, pool, pressure: PressureState, now), ladder_rung(tier) -> Option<&'static str>, ladder_in_flight, set_ladder_dwell}` (a tier's rung steps back up only at GREEN after `dwell` below `low_water`, user decision 2026-09-30 option A), `KvOrchestrator::set_ladder_dwell` (from `reliability.pressure.deescalate_dwell`); `turbine_kv::transfer::TransferPurpose::Compress` (label `compress`; tier → same tier, never observed into path estimates or transfer metrics); metrics `turbine_kv_ladder_rung{tier}`, `turbine_kv_ladder_actions_total{tier,from,to,reason}` (`to="evict"` for `floor_evict`); on the server (6b Task 16) `reason="no_room"` (`turbine_server::kv_orchestrator::LADDER_NO_ROOM`) counts a rewrite whose store found no slot of the new format, eviction reasons `compressed` / `ladder_floor`, drop reason `ladder_floor`; log event `kv_ladder`. Ladder back-off (6b Task 16 follow-up, user decision "6b Task 16: ladder proof results — four open points", 3 A): `turbine_kv::tier::KvTier::room_epoch(&self) -> u64` (required; changes when a slab empties or is released or capacity grows back; `MemTier` on every release), `turbine_kv::metrics::LadderReason::NoRoomBackoff` (`reason="no_room_backoff"`, once per back-off), `HierarchyStats::ladder_backoff_skips`, test hook `MemTier::inject_full(n)`.

Copy ahead (6b, `p6b-copyahead`; user decision 2026-10-01 "6b: shared-prefix eval never demotes the prefix to L1", A): `turbine_kv::hierarchy::{COPY_AHEAD_MAX_FILL (0.5), KvStats.copy_aheads}`, `KvDirectory::has_child_in(key, tier)`, `KvMetrics::{copy_ahead(to), copy_ahead_value(to)}`; metric `turbine_kv_copy_ahead_total{to}` (`to` ∈ l1, l2); log event `kv_copy_ahead` (DEBUG, `reason="copy_ahead"`). At GREEN and YELLOW only, capacity demotion (`demote_to(.., EvictReason::Capacity)`) and `pressure_reclaim` copy unreferenced shared-prefix blocks (≥ 2 children, or an ancestor of one) held in L0 by a resident child into the L0 demotion target while their L0 copy stays; no new config key.

Queued-prefix demotion (6b, `p6b-queued-demote`; user decisions 2026-10-01 "6b: after the held-prefix ledger fix — tail latency and queued prefixes", 1 B, and "6b: queued-prefix demotion — granularity and scope", 1 A, 2 A): `KvHierarchy::{take_queued_prefix_demand() -> usize, detach_prefix(pool, request, &PrefixAttach, alone), pending_blocks(request)}`, `KvStats.queued_prefix_detached`, `KvMetrics.queued_prefix_detached_blocks`; `KvOrchestrator::{attach_again, detach_prefix, take_queued_prefix_demand, pending_blocks}`; `turbine_reliability::admission::AdmissionQueue::behind_head_rev_mut`; `turbine_scheduler::{AdmissionGate::detach_prefixes(pool, want, full_blocks), Scheduler::{detach_queued_prefixes(pool, want) -> Vec<(RequestId, PrefixAttach, usize)>, awaiting_reattach(), reattach(id, PrefixAttach, block_bytes) -> Option<PrefixAttach>, commit_reattached(id, blocks, block_bytes)}, SchedRequest.reattach}`. At YELLOW and ORANGE only, `pressure_reclaim`'s shortfall (`need` minus the victims found) is the demand; the engine takes it after `end_turn`, capped at `CAPACITY_BATCH` (32) blocks per controller tick, and the requests behind the admission queue's head, last to be admitted first, release their whole prefixes until the blocks only they hold (pool refcount 1) cover it (only with a lower tier: without one `pressure_reclaim` is `demote_to` and records none). A released request's estimate (and the queue's copy) covers its whole KV again, and once pumped it starts only after the engine attached it again through the planner (its own pending keys cleared at release; prompt and cached tokens counted once); the blocks the new attach holds, promotion targets included from the moment they are allocated, are committed against its whole-request reservation so the ledger does not count them both as reserved and as `held`. Metric `turbine_kv_queued_prefix_detached_blocks_total`; log event `kv_queued_prefix_detach` (DEBUG, `reason="queued_prefix"`, `blocks`, `alone`, `cached_tokens`); no new config key. The S-9 KV headroom rule (`Admission::within_headroom`) counts `PoolUsage::in_use` (committed + `held`), like `kv_utilization` (fix found on this branch's lab runs).

Kernel ABI v2.11 KV transcode (6b Task 5, `p6b-t5`): header `turbine_kv_transcode_desc { pages /* host array [num_blocks × layers] of device page addresses, block-major */, k_scales, v_scales /* device F32 [layers] of FP8 pages, NULL = 1 */, coded /* num_blocks slots */, coded_block_bytes, seed, num_blocks, layers, block_tokens, num_kv_heads, head_dim, page_dtype, format, direction }` (the spec's `src_pages` / `dst` / `dst_format` / `src_dtype` read `pages` / `coded` / `format` / `page_dtype`, one descriptor for both directions), `turbine_kv_transcode` / `_supported` / `_impl` (`TURBINE_OP_KV_TRANSCODE` 19); Rust `OpKind::KvTranscode` (`abi_minor` 11), `KvTranscodeFormat::{L0, Fp8E4m3, Tq4, Tq2}` (`abi_code` = `KV_FMT_*`), `KvTranscodeDirection::{Encode, Decode}`, `KvTranscodeConfig { src_format, dst_format, page_dtype, head_dim, num_kv_heads, block_tokens, layers }` (`direction`, `codec`, `page_bytes`), `KvTranscodeContext { cfg, pages: &[DeviceSlice] /* block-major */, coded, coded_block_bytes, seed, k_scales, v_scales, codecs: &dyn KvCodecFns }`, `trait KvCodecFns { encode, decode }` (the codec table the cpu-reference provider runs; a library ignores it, so `turbine-kernels` stays free of `turbine-kv`), `KvTranscodeKernel`, `KernelProvider::kv_transcode`, `KernelRegistry::kv_transcode`, `OpConfig::KvTranscode`, `ffi::{KvTranscodeDesc, V21Symbols.kv_transcode}`; CPU `cpu_kv_transcode_ref`; HIP `turbine_hip_fp8` (`kernels/rocm/src/kv_transcode.hip`: FP8 e4m3 over BF16 pages, byte-exact encode and bit-exact decode against `turbine_kv::codec::fp8_e4m3`; FP8 pages are the codec's copy path, not a transcode; TurboQuant follows in Task 8 as `turbine_hip_tq`). Tests: `ffi::tests::optional_groups_v211` (stubs `TURBINE_STUB_GFX942_V211`, `_V211_PARTIAL`, `_V211_NOQUANT`), `abi_header_neutral header_declares_the_v211_kv_transcode_group`, lab `hip_ops::kv_transcode_matches_cpu`.

Kernel ABI v2.11 TurboQuant transcode (6b Task 8, `p6b-t8`; user decision 2026-09-30 "TurboQuant tables through the transcode descriptor", A): header `turbine_tq_params { uint64_t seed; const float *codebooks[4] /* device F32, 2 / 4 / 8 / 16 centroids of 1 … 4 bits */; const float *tables /* device F32 [layers][num_kv_heads][2 × head_dim]: K signs, then V signs (K is TurboQuant_mse since user decision 2026-10-01 "6b Task 9: TurboQuant K quantizer" A; the QJL S is gone) */ }` (shared with the paged attention over TurboQuant pages, Task 12) and the trailing `turbine_kv_transcode_desc.tq_params` (host pointer, read during the call; required by TQ4 / TQ2, ignored by FP8_E4M3, NULL in a probe), still minor 11; Rust `KvTranscodeTables<'a> { codebooks: [TensorView; 4], tables: TensorView }` (`head_elems(head_dim)`), `KvTranscodeKernel::execute_with_tables(ctx, Option<&KvTranscodeTables>)` (default: `execute` when `None`, else refused; `execute` of a TurboQuant format on a library is refused as needing the tables; `KvTranscodeContext` is unchanged, so FP8 callers compile as before), `ffi::TqParamsDesc`; the shim checks the codebooks (dense F32 `[2^bits]`) and `tables` (dense F32 `[layers × num_kv_heads × head_elems]`) and passes `ctx.seed` as `tq_params.seed`; the cpu-reference provider ignores the tables (it runs `ctx.codecs`). HIP `turbine_hip_tq` (`kernels/rocm/src/kv_transcode_tq.hip`, BF16 pages, head_dim 128; refuses tables of another seed than the call's). The server upload of the tables at startup is not part of Task 8. Tests: `ffi::tests::optional_groups_v211` (tables required and shape-checked), `abi_header_neutral header_declares_the_v211_kv_transcode_group` (the struct and the trailing field), `shim::tests` descriptor sizes (`stub_desc_size(20)` = `turbine_tq_params`), lab `hip_ops::kv_transcode_matches_cpu` (`tq_transcode_cases`, `tq_transcode_timing`).

Kernel ABI v2.11 mixed-format paged attention (6b Task 12, `p6b-t12`; user decisions 2026-10-01 "6b Task 12: how per-layer TurboQuant tables reach the paged-attention call" A and "6b Task 10: TurboQuant prefill attention" A): header `turbine_attention_paged_desc` gains, after `k_scale, v_scale`, `const uint8_t *block_formats` (device `[num_seqs, max_blocks_per_seq]` `TURBINE_KVFMT_*` codes laid out like `block_table`, NULL = every block in `dtype`; block `b`'s bytes are the first page bytes of its format at `kv_layer + b ×` the page bytes of `dtype`) and `const struct turbine_tq_params *tq_params` (host, read during the call; `tables` is this layer's `[num_kv_heads][2·head_dim + head_dim²]` slice, required when `dtype` is TQ4 / TQ2 or `block_formats` is not NULL); `TURBINE_DTYPE_TQ4` 18, `TURBINE_DTYPE_TQ2` 19 (pages of `num_kv_heads × block_tokens` records). Rust: `PagedAttentionContext.block_formats: Option<TensorView<'a>>` (U8, provider memory; `Some` takes the mixed implementations: the shim runs the library's own choice unless the bound implementation supports the descriptor), `TqPaged { params, encode, seed, device: Option<KvTranscodeTables<'a>> }` (`device`: the layer's slice; `None` on the CPU provider), `ffi::AttentionPagedDesc.{block_formats, tq_params}`; the shim refuses TurboQuant pages or a table below minor 11 and checks the device tables (`[num_kv_heads · head_elems]`); `turbine_model::executor::TqDeviceTables { codebooks, tables, layers, kv_heads }` (`upload(mem, &TqKv)`, `layer(l, head_dim)`) and `DecoderExecutor::set_tq_device_tables` (the server passes the device copy before the first forward). HIP implementations (impl table): decode `turbine_hip_mixed` (own kernel, `paged_attention_mixed.hip`: TurboQuant blocks in the rotated domain, batch-invariant key splits, BF16 / FP8 blocks as `bf16(e4m3 · scale)`); prefill `ck_tile_fmha_pagedkv_mixed_staged` (pages of a multiple of 128: blocks decoded into a BF16 staging pool, CK pagedkv), `turbine_hip_mixed_staged` (the same with the Turbine BF16 kernel), `turbine_hip_mixed`; `supports` needs head_dim 128, block_tokens a multiple of 16 and a GQA group ≤ 8; the BF16 and FP8 implementations refuse TurboQuant pages and a `block_formats` table (BF16-only tables keep the CK choice). The append writes each row in its block's format (TurboQuant through `tq_device.hpp`'s encode, bit-exact with the codec). `cpu::tq_attention` reads FP8 blocks as `bf16(e4m3 · scale)`. Tests: `abi_header_neutral header_declares_the_v211_mixed_paged_attention_fields`, `cpu::tq_attention::tests::fp8_blocks_read_as_bf16`, lab `hip_ops::paged_mixed_matches_cpu`, `paged_mixed_timings` (perf tier).

TurboQuant tables in the server (6b Task 8 follow-up, `p6b-tqtables`; user decisions "6b Task 8: TurboQuant tables through the transcode descriptor" A and "6b Task 12: how per-layer TurboQuant tables reach the paged-attention call" A): `turbine-server/src/tq_device.rs` (`host_tables(seed, layout) -> TqKv`, `upload(mem, seed, layout) -> TqDeviceTables`, `transcode_view(&TqDeviceTables) -> KvTranscodeTables`, `table_bytes(layout)`, `needs_tables(formats, layout)`, `reserved_bytes(cfg, layout)`, `install(tq, mem, executor)`); `turbine_model::executor::ModelExecutor::set_tq_device_tables(TqDeviceTables) -> Result<(), ModelError>` (default ignores; `DecoderExecutor` forwards to its inherent method); `PreparedModel.tq_table_bytes` (workspace pool, next to `transcode_staging_bytes`). The seed is the pool's KV namespace seed (`HostCodec::of`), so the transcode, the attention and the CPU codec agree. Event `tq_tables` (executor upload), `kv_transcode_device` names `tq4`/`tq2` when the library serves them, `kv_transcode_tq_tables_failed` (upload failed: host codec). `TIER_FORMAT_REFUSALS` `tq4`/`tq2` are `experimental`; rows `amd/gfx1201/{LlamaForCausalLM,OlmoeForCausalLM}/bf16/{tq4,tq2}/none` are `experimental` until the S-8 gate. `kv_format_availability` refuses `kv.dtype tq4|tq2` on a GPU backend with `kv_tq_unavailable` only when the loaded library lacks the v2.11 group. Tests: `turbine-server --bin turbine-server tq_device::` and `kv_orchestrator::tests::tq_transcode_receives_the_codec_tables`, `--test tiny_server tq_tables_are_in_the_workspace_pool tq_tier_transcodes_on_the_device_path tq_kv_serves_on_cpu`; lab `--test kv_gpu lossy_tier_reuse_tq4 tq_kv_serves_on_the_device`.

Kernel ABI v2.11 host-to-device copy kernel (6b, `p6b-copykernel`; user decision 2026-10-02 "6b: KV promotions slow decode — which fix" A; v2.11 had not shipped, so no minor bump): header `turbine_copy_seg { void *dst; const void *src; size_t bytes; }` and `int32_t turbine_memcpy_h2d_kernel(turbine_ctx *ctx, turbine_stream *s, const turbine_copy_seg *segs, int32_t count, int32_t workgroups)` (optional, resolved at minor ≥ 11 with the v2.5 group, apart from the transcode trio; `workgroups` 0 = the library's choice, 2 on the ROCm shim; sources inside pinned memory of the context; bit-exact for any length and alignment); HIP `kernels/rocm/src/copy_kernel.hip`. Rust `ffi::{CopySeg, CopyKernelFn, V21Symbols.copy_kernel}`; `turbine_tensor::CopyEngine::{has_copy_kernel() -> bool (default false), copy_async_batch_kernel(&[CopyOp]) -> Result<Vec<CopyTicket>, MemoryError> (default Unsupported; pinned → device ops only, one ticket, no compute fence)}`, implemented by `ShimContext`; `turbine_core::config::{KvTransferConfig.promotion_copy, PromotionCopy::{Sdma, Kernel}}`; `CopyStreamBackend::set_promotion_copy(PromotionCopy) -> Result<(), String>` (refusal `promotion_copy_kernel_unavailable`; log `event="kv_promotion_copy"`). Stub `TURBINE_STUB_GFX942_V211` exports it (memcpy; hooks `stub_h2d_kernel_calls`, `stub_h2d_kernel_segs`). Tests: `shim::tests::a_kernel_copy_batch_is_one_kernel_call`, `abi_header_neutral header_declares_the_v211_copy_kernel`, `kv_orchestrator::tests::promotions_use_the_copy_kernel_when_configured`, lab `turbine-kernels --test lab pinned_copy_kernel_is_bit_exact`.

Resolved RoPE reporting (P6a follow-up, lead rotation r12): `turbine_model::config::{RopeSummary { theta, rope_type, factor, attention_factor }, ModelArchConfig::rope_summary()}` (`rope_type` one of `default`, `llama3`, `yarn`; `factor` and `attention_factor` `None` outside a scaled / YaRN config respectively); logged once per rank at model load as `event="rope_config"` (INFO, `turbine-server::model::log_rope_config`); `/turbine/v1/status` `model.rope { theta, rope_type, factor, attention_factor }` (`backend::ModelStatus.rope`, `backend::ModelBackend.rope`).
